Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The reactions operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 410. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 218 with 5 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to reactions structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 410/218 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.
